\section{Context, archive, and purpose}
\label{sec:newv1-context}

The objective is a rigorous construction of four-dimensional
quantum Yang--Mills theory for a compact nonabelian gauge group and
a proof that its physical Hamiltonian has a strictly positive gap
above the vacuum.  The intended route passes from exact
gauge-compatible finite regulators through a volume-uniform
renormalization construction, a cofinal all-order positive
observable state, removal of the regulators, reflection-positive
Osterwalder--Schrader reconstruction, and quantitative Euclidean
clustering strong enough to imply a Hamiltonian spectral gap.

That endpoint has not been reached.  The archive contains rigorous
finite-stage identities, quantitative estimates, conditional
implications, and exact no-go results.  The purpose of this compact
restart is to make the live dependency chain trackable without
discarding those proofs.  This V1 first summarizes the major
mathematical acquisitions and their claim boundaries.  It then
continues the current finite-dimensional bottleneck: the
constraint-retaining covariant Loewner pencil.

\begin{record}[Frozen predecessor]
\label{rec:newv1-archive}
The completed preceding compact lineage is frozen as
\[
\texttt{Renewal\_Yang\_Mills\_Mass\_Gap\_Research\_Notes\_V100\_f9.tex}.
\]
Its validated record is
