\end{array}                                                   \tag{1.1}
\]
Earlier files with suffixes \(\texttt{\_f1},\ldots,\texttt{\_f8}\)
remain proof archives.  Successors of this V1 append to the complete
active source and replace the immediate active predecessor only
after validation.  Every fifth version audits the then-current
Standard-Model--Einstein and Navier--Stokes notes.  At V100 the
active file is frozen with the next unused suffix and another
contextual V1 begins.
\end{record}

\begin{firewall}[No mass-gap claim]
\label{fire:newv1-no-gap}
No four-dimensional continuum Yang--Mills measure or Hamiltonian
mass gap has been proved.  In particular, the current
finite-dimensional response estimates do not replace construction
of the full interacting state, reflection positivity, regulator
removal, clustering, or the spectral argument.
\end{firewall}

\section{Major mathematical results acquired so far}
\label{sec:newv1-summary}

This section is a proof-status map.  Each statement retains the
hypotheses of its archived source; summarizing a conditional theorem
does not populate its hypotheses.

\subsection{Finite-cutoff construction and permanent no-go results}

\begin{record}[Constructive identities]
\label{rec:newv1-constructive}
The archive proves, on their declared finite regulators:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=3.3em]
\item Peter--Weyl and heat-kernel decompositions, exact Duhamel and
forest interpolation identities, endpoint-factorizing BKAR
representations, and fixed-factor associative block pushforwards;
\item exact retained/eliminated disintegrations, finite-range cochain
liftings, a reduced parity-tree fibre, stationary conditional
centres, and energy-orthogonal Gaussian interpolation;
\item gauge-compatible score, Ward, current, determinant, toron, and
topological-sector ledgers, including the required higher connected
cumulants in differentiated currents; and
\item degree-faithful analytic forests in which the unavoidable
vertex factors \(\prod_v d(v)!\) are summed by an exact
Pruefer-word identity rather than suppressed.
\end{enumerate}
\end{record}

\begin{record}[Shortcuts rigorously excluded]
\label{rec:newv1-permanent-nogos}
The following replacements have been disproved in the archive:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=3.3em]
\item an unconditional small bad-set probability does not imply a
uniform conditional influence row;
\item pair covariance does not determine the higher conditional
cumulants needed by differentiated nonlinear currents;
\item gauge gradients, torons, retained modes, and other massless
directions cannot be hidden in a uniformly gapped eliminated
determinant;
\item contraction cannot include the calibrated marginal and
redundant directions on which the exact RG derivative has unit
pullback; and
\item reflection positivity or static decay alone does not identify
a continuum Hamiltonian gap.
\end{enumerate}
\end{record}

\subsection{Local vacuum, polymer, and stable-slice structure}

The reduced filling construction gives local coercivity and
identifies the unique moving vacuum over coarse-flat or commuting
toron backgrounds.  Connected bad block sets pay a Wilson action
proportional to their size.  The soft-good selector is absorbed
exactly into a stronger product reference, leaving a nonnegative
soft-bad insertion.  The far-bad activity is stretched
exponentially small and the soft activity is \(O(\beta^{-1/5})\).
The hard-core four-mark polymer theorem has an explicit closing
condition once the full owned activity is small.

The same lineage constructs analytic Morse charts, exact
Morse--Gaussian gluing without cutoff derivatives, regular collars,
triangular colour-saturation schedules, measured-coordinate
Jacobians, and calibrated contraction after removal of marginal and
redundant rows.  These are finite-stage or conditional components;
one common regulator has not yet populated every input needed for
iteration to the continuum.

\subsection{Exact reduced symbol and response oracle}

\begin{record}[Coefficient-exact finite fibre]
\label{rec:newv1-fibre}
The parity-cell incidence construction reduces the relevant
gauge-fixed symbol to \(45\) coordinates inside the
\(64\)-dimensional parity-edge fibre.  Exact noncommutative colour
reduction, harmonic routers, dual-colour compression, sparse toron
arrays, translated quartic and cubic vertices, and the physical
re-tree maps have been constructed coefficient by coefficient.

The endpoint-minus-Wilson determinant and its first two axial jets
are exact.  The later Laurent lift supplies complete Hessian,
quartic, and routed cubic banks.  Complete transported trace scalars
obey the proved symmetries; raw fixed-tree matrices are not silently
identified across axes or reflections.
\end{record}

\begin{record}[Hessian positivity and response envelopes]
\label{rec:newv1-hessian}
On the full momentum torus, the two reduced Hessians have the
rigorous floors
\[
 H_{\rm W}(k)\succeq \frac{49}{1250}I,\qquad
 H_*(k)\succeq \frac{343}{1250}I.                          \tag{1.2}
\]
The endpoint improvement is proved through the sixteen-alias
reciprocal reduction, not inferred from sampling.

Exact Gram-relative inverse recursions and trace-ideal inequalities
give finite pointwise response envelopes through external order two.
Those envelopes are far too large for the desired response sign,
but they are rigorous and localize the losses in covariance
derivatives and vertex transport.  The coefficientwise covariant
recompiles of V74 and V76 are valid but larger than the earlier
V69 majorant; they remain recorded no-go results for taking
Frobenius sums before closed-trace cancellation.
\end{record}

\subsection{Flat connections, dressed anchors, and torus covers}

The normalized alias frame \(V(k)\) is an isometry and has the flat
diagonal connection
\[
 \Gamma_\mu=-\frac{i}{2}\operatorname{diag}(s_\mu),\qquad
 {\boldsymbol\nabla}_\mu X
 =\partial_\mu X-[X,\Gamma_\mu].                           \tag{1.3}
\]
Connection commutators telescope in every closed trace word,
including typed cycles whose factors map between different routed
fibres.  For the Hessians,
\[
 {\boldsymbol\nabla}_\mu H_e
 =V^*(\partial_\mu{\cal M}_e)V.                            \tag{1.4}
\]
This is an exact identity, not a norm estimate.

Residual-dressed rational inverse certificates have been constructed
at the sixteen dyadic anchors and then at all \(256\) quarter-turn
anchors.  Coefficient-exact Laurent variation gives a genuine, but
coarse, full-torus dressed-vertex cover and hence finite global
response envelopes.  Fixed-anchor absolute transport was populated
and proved noncompetitive, so that architecture is closed.

\subsection{The immediate V97--V100 frontier}

\begin{record}[Ordinary and covariant Loewner grid theorems]
\label{rec:newv1-loewner}
For the active external direction and all quarter-turn points,
\[
 2H_e\pm\partial_1H_e\succ0.                               \tag{1.5}
\]
The \(1024\) exact rational congruence certificates have common
floors
\[
 \delta_{\rm W}=0.020672571033,\qquad
 \delta_*=0.282508640785.                                  \tag{1.6}
\]
Consequently the first normalized ordinary covariance derivative
has grid operator norm below \(2\).

For the covariant pencils,
\[
 \widetilde P_e^\sigma
 :=2H_e+\sigma{\boldsymbol\nabla}_1H_e,\qquad
 \sigma\in\{-1,1\},                                       \tag{1.7}
\]
all \(1024\) grid certificates also succeed, with larger common
floors
\[
\begin{split}
\widetilde\delta_{\rm W}
 &=1511577209220281/30000006397560000,\\
\widetilde\delta_*
 &=9474024992694083/7500001364572500.
\end{split}                                                 \tag{1.8}
\]
They are approximately \(0.050386\) and \(1.263203\).
\end{record}

\begin{record}[Two exact continuation obstructions]
\label{rec:newv1-latest-nogos}
An individual fine curl block has a moving gauge kernel.  At
\(k=0\), alias \((0,1,0,0)\), both signs of
\(2{\cal M}_e\pm\partial_1{\cal M}_e\) have exact negative
Gaussian-rational witnesses.  Independent \(4\times4\) alias-block
positivity is therefore false; the \(45\)-dimensional range
constraint is load-bearing.

Connection removal lowers the covariant Laurent stock and enlarges
the common certified anchor radii to
\[
 0.000853815031\quad({\rm W}),\qquad
 0.000488279171\quad(*).                                   \tag{1.9}
\]
Both are still much smaller than the quarter-cell width \(\pi/4\).
Thus neither the ordinary nor covariant pencil can be globalized by
combining one common grid floor with one scalar coefficient stock.
\end{record}

\subsection{Terminal requirements beyond the present calculation}

\begin{assessment}[Full dependency boundary]
\label{ass:newv1-dependency}
Even a full-torus response sign would settle only one finite-stage
input.  A complete mass-gap proof still requires:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\arabic*)},leftmargin=3.3em]
\item a volume-uniform nonlinear good/bad expansion and a calibrated
stable-slice RG contraction for one common gauge-invariant regulator;
\item controlled marginal flow, topological sectors, determinant
phases, and compatible fixed-factor iteration across all scales;
\item a cofinal all-order positive gauge-invariant observable ledger,
because no fixed finite correlation order determines an interacting
state;
\item construction and regulator removal of a reflection-positive
continuum measure with the required locality and symmetry identities;
\item Osterwalder--Schrader reconstruction, vacuum control, and a
uniform Euclidean clustering exponent; and
\item transfer of that exponent to a strictly positive spectral gap
in the physical continuum Hamiltonian.
\end{enumerate}
None of these gates is declared acquired merely from the
finite-dimensional work below.
\end{assessment}

\section{Continuation: constraint-multiplier completion}
\label{sec:newv1-completion}

The V98 obstruction shows exactly why positivity must be imposed
after restricting the alias direct sum.  This section replaces the
failed independent-block requirement by an equivalent full-space
certificate containing explicit constraint multipliers.

Let
\[
 {\cal E}=\bigoplus_{\epsilon\in\{0,1\}^4}\mathbb C^4
 \cong\mathbb C^{64}.                                      \tag{1.10}
\]
Let \(R:\mathbb C^{45}\to\mathbb C^{64}\) select the retained V21
parity edges and let \(R_0:\mathbb C^{19}\to\mathbb C^{64}\)
select the omitted edges.  After the fixed coordinate ordering,
\((R\ R_0)\) is unitary.  With the normalized parity Fourier
transform \(F(k)\), define
\[
 V(k)=F(k)R,\qquad W(k)=F(k)R_0,\qquad
 U(k)=(V(k)\ W(k)).                                        \tag{1.11}
\]
Then \(U(k)\) is unitary,
\[
 V^*V=I_{45},\quad W^*W=I_{19},\quad V^*W=0,               \tag{1.12}
\]
and
\[
 C(k):=W(k)^*,\qquad
 \ker C(k)=\Ran V(k).                                      \tag{1.13}
\]

For an action and sign, put
\[
 B_e^\sigma(k)
 :=
 \operatorname{diag}_\epsilon
 \left(2{\cal M}_{e,\epsilon}
       +\sigma\partial_1{\cal M}_{e,\epsilon}\right).
                                                                  \tag{1.14}
\]
The constrained pencil is
\[
 \widetilde P_e^\sigma(k)=V(k)^*B_e^\sigma(k)V(k).         \tag{1.15}
\]

\begin{theorem}[Exact constraint-multiplier completion]
\label{thm:newv1-multiplier}
Let \(V:\mathbb C^r\to\mathbb C^n\) and
\(W:\mathbb C^m\to\mathbb C^n\) be isometries such that
\(U=(V\ W)\) is unitary, and put \(C=W^*\).
For any Hermitian \(B\in M_n(\mathbb C)\) and any
\(\delta\ge0\), the following are equivalent:
\[
\begin{array}{ll}
\textup{(i)}&V^*BV\succeq\delta I_r,\\[2mm]
\textup{(ii)}&
\text{there exists }Z\in M_{n\times m}(\mathbb C)\text{ such that}\\
&\hspace{12mm}B+ZC+C^*Z^*\succeq\delta I_n.
\end{array}                                                  \tag{1.16}
\]
Thus constrained positivity is equivalent to unconstrained
positivity after addition of a term that vanishes on
\(\ker C=\Ran V\).
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Write the block matrix in the unitary coordinates of \(U\):
\[
 U^*BU=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A&E\\ E^*&D
 \end{pmatrix},
 \qquad A=V^*BV.                                           \tag{1.17}
\]
Since \(CU=(0\ I_m)\), define \(Z\) by
\[
 U^*Z=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 -E\\[1mm](\delta I_m-D)/2
 \end{pmatrix}.                                             \tag{1.18}
\]
Direct multiplication gives the exact completion
\[
\begin{split}
 U^*(B+ZC+C^*Z^*)U
 &=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A&E\\E^*&D
 \end{pmatrix}
 +
 \begin{pmatrix}
 0&-E\\-E^*&\delta I_m-D
 \end{pmatrix}\\
 &=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A&0\\0&\delta I_m
 \end{pmatrix}.
\end{split}                                                  \tag{1.19}
\]
If (i) holds, (1.19) proves (ii).

Conversely, let \(x\in\mathbb C^r\).  Because \(CVx=0\),
\[
 x^*V^*(B+ZC+C^*Z^*)Vx=x^*V^*BVx.                         \tag{1.20}
\]
Restriction of (ii) to \(\Ran V\) proves (i).
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Correct algebraic target for the alias pencil]
\label{cor:newv1-alias-target}
For each \(e,\sigma,k\),
\[
 \widetilde P_e^\sigma(k)\succeq0
\]
if and only if there is a multiplier
\(Z_e^\sigma(k):\mathbb C^{19}\to{\cal E}\) such that
\[
 B_e^\sigma(k)
 +Z_e^\sigma(k)C(k)
 +C(k)^*Z_e^\sigma(k)^*\succeq0.                           \tag{1.21}
\]
The equivalence preserves every positive floor \(\delta\).
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Apply Theorem~\ref{thm:newv1-multiplier} to (1.11)--(1.15).
\end{proof}

At every quarter-turn anchor, V99 and
Corollary~\ref{cor:newv1-alias-target} guarantee a multiplier with
the exact floors (1.8).  This does not yet produce one coherent
Laurent or interval multiplier on the whole torus.  Formula (1.18)
is existentially exact but contains the already-compressed block
\(A\); using it without an independent estimate of \(A\) would be
circular.  The useful next step is therefore to find a sparse
multiplier from the explicit tree constraints which cancels the
moving-kernel cross terms before any unknown compressed floor is
inserted.

\begin{decision}[V2 constructs a noncircular multiplier ansatz]
\label{dec:newv1-next}
Expand \(C(k)=R_0^*F(k)^*\) and the direct-sum pencil
\(B_e^\sigma(k)\) exactly.  Use the nineteen omitted parity edges to
construct the sparsest Laurent multiplier \(Z_e^\sigma(k)\) that
cancels the kernel--range cross terms responsible for the V98
witness.  Test the augmented matrix (1.21) first on the complete
quarter-turn grid, then symbolically by alias type.

If a proposed multiplier fails, retain an exact rational negative
vector and revise the constraint ansatz upstream.  If it succeeds on
the grid, seek a full-torus sum-of-squares or interval Schur
certificate for the augmented matrix.  Do not use the existential
formula (1.18) as a proof of its own upper-left block, and do not
return to the scalar common-radius schemes closed in V97 and V100.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in the new V1]
\label{ass:newv1-progress}
The archive has been compressed into an explicit proof-status map,
and the immediate alias obstruction has been repaired at the level
of logic.  Theorem~\ref{thm:newv1-multiplier} converts positivity on
the load-bearing \(45\)-dimensional range into an equivalent
full-space constraint-multiplier certificate.  The remaining task is
constructive rather than definitional: produce a noncircular
multiplier whose augmented symbol is positive for every momentum.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after new V1]
\label{fire:newv1-final}
No such full-torus multiplier has yet been constructed.
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv1-multiplier} is an exact finite-dimensional
equivalence, not a proof that the present covariant pencil is
globally positive.  No continuum response sign, interacting
Yang--Mills measure, reflection-positive regulator removal,
exponential clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap follows at this
stage.
\end{firewall}

\section{V2: one-coordinate block certificates and exact upstream no-go results}
\label{sec:newv2-block}

\subsection{Why the V1 multiplier search is being moved upstream}

The V1 decision asked for a sparse multiplier on the full
\(64\)-dimensional alias fibre.  Before introducing nineteen
momentum-dependent multiplier columns, one should use all structure
already present in the compressed \(45\)-dimensional pencil.  The
exact Laurent bank has degree one in each active coordinate.  Hence
one momentum coordinate can be removed by a finite block
positivity certificate.  This is a noncircular alternative to
substituting the existential multiplier (1.18): its auxiliary
matrix must be exhibited and checked independently.

Fix the first coordinate, write
\[
 \vartheta=(k_2,k_3,k_4),\qquad z=e^{ik_1},
\]
and let
\[
 P_{e,\sigma}(z;\vartheta)
 =2H_e(k)+\sigma\nabla_1H_e(k),\qquad \sigma\in\{-1,1\}.   \tag{2.1}
\]
Here
\[
 \nabla_1H=\partial_1H+[H,\Gamma_1],
 \qquad
 \Gamma_1=-\frac{i}{2}\operatorname{diag}(s_1),           \tag{2.2}
\]
is the proved row-and-column covariant derivative of the reduced
parity-edge fibre.

\begin{proposition}[Exact active-coordinate degree]
\label{prop:newv2-degree}
For every \(e,\sigma,\vartheta\), there are exactly computable
matrices
\[
 A_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)=A_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)^*,
 \qquad B_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)\in M_{45}(\mathbb C)
\]
such that
\[
 P_{e,\sigma}(z;\vartheta)
 =A_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)
  +zB_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)
  +\bar zB_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)^*.                       \tag{2.3}
\]
Their entries are Gaussian-rational Laurent polynomials in the
three transverse phases.  No coefficient with active exponent of
absolute value greater than one occurs.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
Every row of the V21 incidence factor joins parity edges in one
fundamental cell or its translate by a single coordinate vector.
Consequently every exponent difference in its Gram matrix has first
component in \(\{-1,0,1\}\).  The endpoint completion is another
Gram matrix of row differences with the same property.  Multiplying
the coefficient at exponent \(\alpha\) by the covariant first-jet
factor
\[
 i\left(\alpha_1-\frac{s_{i,1}-s_{j,1}}2\right)           \tag{2.4}
\]
does not change its support.  Group the terms with
\(\alpha_1=0,1,-1\).  Hermiticity of (2.1) identifies the
\(-1\) coefficient with the adjoint of the \(+1\) coefficient,
which gives (2.3).  The exact support enumeration and adjoint check
are repeated by
\[
 \texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v2\_fejer\_midpoint\_counterexample.py}.
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Elementary one-coordinate block certificate]
\label{thm:newv2-block}
Let
\[
 P(z)=A+zB+\bar zB^*,\qquad |z|=1,\quad A=A^*,
\]
and let \(\delta\in\mathbb R\).  If there is a Hermitian \(X\) for
which
\[
 {\cal Q}_{\delta,X}:=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A-X-\frac{\delta}{2}I&B\\
 B^*&X-\frac{\delta}{2}I
 \end{pmatrix}\succeq0,                                  \tag{2.5}
\]
then
\[
 P(z)\succeq\delta I\qquad\text{for every }|z|=1.          \tag{2.6}
\]
Moreover, if an explicit two-term spectral factor
\[
 P(z)-\delta I=(G_0+zG_1)^*(G_0+zG_1)                    \tag{2.7}
\]
is supplied, then (2.5) holds with
\[
 X=G_1^*G_1+\frac{\delta}{2}I.                            \tag{2.8}
\]
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
For \(u\in\mathbb C^n\), put \(w_z=(u,zu)\).  Direct multiplication,
using \(|z|=1\), gives
\[
 w_z^*{\cal Q}_{\delta,X}w_z
 =u^*\bigl(P(z)-\delta I\bigr)u.                          \tag{2.9}
\]
Thus (2.5) implies (2.6).  Expanding (2.7) yields
\[
 A-\delta I=G_0^*G_0+G_1^*G_1,\qquad B=G_0^*G_1.         \tag{2.10}
\]
With (2.8), the matrix in (2.5) becomes
\[
 \begin{pmatrix}
 G_0^*G_0&G_0^*G_1\\
 G_1^*G_0&G_1^*G_1
 \end{pmatrix}
 =
 \begin{pmatrix}G_0^*\\G_1^*\end{pmatrix}
 \begin{pmatrix}G_0&G_1\end{pmatrix}\succeq0.             \tag{2.11}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[What has and has not been used]
\label{rem:newv2-fejer}
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv2-block} uses only direct multiplication.
No unproved matrix Fejer--Riesz converse is being imported.  A
future certificate must provide \(X\), or \(G_0,G_1\), by exact
formula or certified enclosure.  The theorem then removes \(k_1\)
without sampling it.
\end{remark}

\subsection{The covariant unit-factor shortcut is false}

Let \({\cal A}_e(k)\) denote the normalized V74 factor whose Gram
matrix is \(H_e(k)\), and let
\[
 {\cal D}_1{\cal A}
 =\partial_1{\cal A}-\Delta_1{\cal A}-{\cal A}\Gamma_1.   \tag{2.12}
\]
The attractive sufficient gate
\[
 ({\cal D}_1{\cal A})^*({\cal D}_1{\cal A})
 \preceq {\cal A}^*{\cal A}                              \tag{2.13}
\]
would prove \(2H\pm\nabla_1H\succeq0\) by Cauchy--Schwarz.
It is nevertheless false.

\begin{countertheorem}[Exact failure of the covariant unit-factor gate]
\label{ctr:newv2-factor}
In the frozen \(45\)-component retained-edge order, (2.13) fails
for both actions.  Exact witnesses occur at
\[
 k_{\mathrm W}=(0,\pi,\pi,0),\qquad
 k_{\mathrm E}=(\pi,\pi,\pi,\pi).                        \tag{2.14}
\]
For the Wilson witness \(x_{\mathrm W}\) and endpoint witness
\(x_{\mathrm E}\) displayed below, exact arithmetic gives
\[
\begin{array}{c|c|c|c}
e&x^*H_ex&
x^*({\cal D}_1{\cal A}_e)^*({\cal D}_1{\cal A}_e)x&
x^*\!\left(H_e-({\cal D}_1{\cal A}_e)^*
({\cal D}_1{\cal A}_e)\right)x\\ \hline
\mathrm W&47650931204&166378010601/2&-71076148193/2\\
\mathrm E&3092067209900/3&6759765329617/4&
-7911027149251/12
\end{array}                                                \tag{2.15}
\]
In particular, the energy ratios are respectively
\[
 \frac{166378010601}{95301862408}>1,\qquad
 \frac{20279295988851}{12368268839600}>1.                \tag{2.16}
\]
\end{countertheorem}

\begin{proof}
Split each vector into six consecutive blocks (the first five have
length eight):
\[
\begin{aligned}
x_{\mathrm W}^{(1)}={}&(-154147,216482,154147,8268,-86758,
151445,-111716,86758),\\
x_{\mathrm W}^{(2)}={}&(-12972,-9648,-206742,347917,283853,
206742,21069,28926),\\
x_{\mathrm W}^{(3)}={}&(-45534,9884,-14200,-69105,45534,
-1233,7953,13538),\\
x_{\mathrm W}^{(4)}={}&(-174843,23048,-41032,246564,174843,
-460,16889,10320),\\
x_{\mathrm W}^{(5)}={}&(-98407,13206,173956,-125773,98407,
-2187,-17765,-13442),\\
x_{\mathrm W}^{(6)}={}&(-234501,30378,391579,319466,234501).
\end{aligned}                                               \tag{2.17}
\]
\[
\begin{aligned}
x_{\mathrm E}^{(1)}={}&(-131645,186175,131645,0,-72791,
127321,-102943,72791),\\
x_{\mathrm E}^{(2)}={}&(0,0,-175734,307379,248525,175734,0,0),\\
x_{\mathrm E}^{(3)}={}&(-62576,109782,-20957,-88496,62576,0,0,0),\\
x_{\mathrm E}^{(4)}={}&(-151072,265037,-50595,213648,151072,0,0,0),\\
x_{\mathrm E}^{(5)}={}&(-83533,146548,146109,-118134,83533,0,0,0),\\
x_{\mathrm E}^{(6)}={}&(-201667,353799,352739,285200,201667).
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.18}
\]
Set \(x_e=(x_e^{(1)},\ldots,x_e^{(6)})^\top\).
At the two corners all phase values are rational signs.  Substitute
the V21 incidence rows into the two Gram forms, use the effective
rate (2.4), and collect rational terms.  The three resulting
quadratic forms are exactly those in (2.15); their last entries are
negative.  This proves the assertion without relying on a floating
eigenvalue.  The independent exact recomputation has canonical
payload SHA--256
\[
\texttt{BEFC0AFE417A8E421BF2A8F9AC5B2A6CE9963140B1A1CF46CDE42AFEE1D59DAF}.
                                                               \tag{2.19}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[The direct pencil survives this no-go]
\label{fire:newv2-factor}
Countertheorem~\ref{ctr:newv2-factor} refutes only the sufficient
factor contraction (2.13).  It does not refute
\(2H_e\pm\nabla_1H_e\succeq0\).  Direct floating searches on the
complete \(5^4\) mesh and \(4096\) seeded random points found no
negative direct pencil; those searches are route-selection evidence,
not a proof.
\end{firewall}

\subsection{The midpoint and every scalar splitting are false}

The first possible use of Theorem~\ref{thm:newv2-block} is
\(X=A/2\) at \(\delta=0\).  This midpoint is not merely unsuccessful:
at a point where \(A\succ0\), it is the best member of the whole
scalar family \(X=tA\).

\begin{lemma}[Midpoint optimality among scalar splittings]
\label{lem:newv2-midpoint-optimal}
Let \(A\succ0\), \(B\in M_n(\mathbb C)\), and
\[
 Q_t=\begin{pmatrix}(1-t)A&B\\B^*&tA\end{pmatrix},
 \qquad 0\le t\le1.                                      \tag{2.20}
\]
If \(Q_{1/2}\not\succeq0\), then \(Q_t\not\succeq0\) for every
\(t\in[0,1]\).
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
Congruence by
\(\operatorname{diag}(A^{-1/2},A^{-1/2})\) changes (2.20) into
\[
 \begin{pmatrix}(1-t)I&T\\T^*&tI\end{pmatrix},
 \qquad T=A^{-1/2}BA^{-1/2}.                              \tag{2.21}
\]
For \(0<t<1\), a Schur complement, or the singular-value
decomposition of \(T\), shows that (2.21) is positive semidefinite
if and only if
\[
 \|T\|_{\mathrm{op}}^2\le t(1-t).                        \tag{2.22}
\]
The right side is at most \(1/4\), with equality only at \(t=1/2\).
At \(t=0,1\), positivity requires \(B=0\), which would also make the
midpoint positive.  The contrapositive proves the lemma.
\end{proof}

\begin{countertheorem}[Exact midpoint obstruction]
\label{ctr:newv2-midpoint}
For both signs \(\sigma\) and both actions, the midpoint block
\[
 Q_{1/2}=
 \begin{pmatrix}A_{e,\sigma}/2&B_{e,\sigma}\\
 B_{e,\sigma}^*&A_{e,\sigma}/2\end{pmatrix}               \tag{2.23}
\]
is indefinite at an explicit transverse corner, even though its
central coefficient \(A_{e,\sigma}\) is positive definite.
For Wilson use
\(\vartheta_{\mathrm W}=(\pi,\pi,0)\); for the endpoint use
\(\vartheta_{\mathrm E}=(\pi,0,0)\).  Certified lower floors for
the two central coefficients are
\[
 \lambda_{\min}(A_{\mathrm W,\sigma})
 \ge\frac{1271821285153607}{5000001074670000}>0,
\quad
 \lambda_{\min}(A_{\mathrm E,\sigma})
 \ge\frac{16562836882755601}{5000001088935000}>0.         \tag{2.24}
\]
Nevertheless,
\[
 (w_{\mathrm W}^{+})^*Q_{1/2}w_{\mathrm W}^{+}
 =-\frac{1406618239902521}{3}<0,\qquad
 (w_{\mathrm E}^{+})^*Q_{1/2}w_{\mathrm E}^{+}
 =-9006912167092030<0.                                   \tag{2.25}
\]
Here the superscript \(+\) denotes \(\sigma=1\); the
\(\sigma=-1\) witnesses are the complex conjugates.  Consequently
no scalar splitting \(X=tA_{e,\sigma}\) can prove the pencils at
these corners.
\end{countertheorem}

\begin{proof}
Index the two blocks of (2.23) by \(0,\ldots,89\), with the first
copy occupying \(0,\ldots,44\).  All unlisted components below are
zero.  The Wilson witness is
\[
\begin{array}{c|r@{}l}
j&\multicolumn{2}{c}{(w_{\mathrm W}^{+})_j}\\ \hline
25&-23520803&+5880201i\\
41&-31114590&+7778648i\\
45&-18155123&+9682732i\\
46& 21729822&-11589239i\\
47& 18155123&-9682732i\\
55&-26852540&+14321355i\\
56& 33764773&-18007879i\\
57& 26996285&-14398019i\\
58& 26852540&-14321355i
\end{array}                                                \tag{2.26}
\]
and the endpoint witness is
\[
\begin{array}{c|r@{}l}
j&\multicolumn{2}{c}{(w_{\mathrm E}^{+})_j}\\ \hline
41&-26298743&+7812484i\\
51&-23774142&+14073217i\\
55&-25756205&+15246478i\\
56& 24111189&-14272947i\\
57& 23774142&-14073217i\\
58& 25756205&-15246478i
\end{array}.                                               \tag{2.27}
\]
At the declared transverse corners, group the exact Laurent
coefficients as in (2.3).  Their \(-1\) and \(+1\) groups are exact
adjoints.  Exact rational congruence/Gershgorin certificates give
(2.24).  Direct Gaussian-integer substitution of (2.26)--(2.27)
into (2.23) gives (2.25).  Changing \(\sigma\) conjugates all
coefficients and the two witnesses, so the same real quadratic
values result.  The executable recomputation has canonical payload
SHA--256
\[
\texttt{E11400C46B5DA43454E1A5C5229C88080A0EBCAA9494D2F730FEABEAA86B8169}.
                                                               \tag{2.28}
\]
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv2-midpoint-optimal} then excludes every
\(X=tA\).
\end{proof}

\begin{decision}[V3 must construct a noncommutative splitting]
\label{dec:newv2-next}
Retain the degree-one reduction (2.3)--(2.5), but abandon both the
unit-factor contraction and scalar splittings.  At each exact
transverse anchor, construct a genuinely matrix-valued Hermitian
\(X_{e,\sigma}(\vartheta)\) by a Riccati, spectral-factor, or
primal--dual semidefinite certificate.  First certify one anchor
exactly.  Then exploit covariance to transport the certificate and
seek a three-variable Laurent or interval enclosure of \(X\).

If no such \(X\) exists at an anchor, (2.5) cannot decide the direct
pencil there and the route returns to the nineteen-constraint
multiplier of V1.  If it does exist, one full torus coordinate has
been removed exactly and the remaining problem is three-dimensional.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V2]
\label{ass:newv2-progress}
V2 has replaced an unconstrained search for nineteen multiplier
columns by a precise smaller certificate problem.  It proves the
one-coordinate block implication, proves that the covariant
unit-factor shortcut is false, and proves that every scalar block
splitting fails.  The surviving target is therefore unambiguous:
an exact noncommutative \(45\times45\) splitting \(X\), or an exact
spectral factor, for the direct covariant pencil.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V2]
\label{fire:newv2-final}
No global \(X\), spectral factor, full-torus covariant Loewner
bound, continuum response sign, interacting Yang--Mills measure,
reflection-positive regulator removal, exponential clustering, or
Hamiltonian mass gap has yet been proved.  The exact negative
witnesses above close only two insufficient proof devices; they do
not constitute evidence against the direct pencil.
\end{firewall}

\section{V3: exact noncommutative splittings on two active circles}
\label{sec:newv3-circles}

\subsection{Candidate construction and exact acceptance}

The midpoint obstruction in V2 does not obstruct
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv2-block}; it says that \(X\) must distinguish
matrix directions.  For a fixed pair \(A,B\), the two boundary
Riccati equations suggested by the Schur complements are
\[
 X+B X^{-1}B^*=A,\qquad
 Y+B^*Y^{-1}B=A.                                         \tag{3.1}
\]
Floating iteration from \(A\) gives candidate maximal solutions
\(X_{\max}\) and \(Y_{\max}\), and hence the candidate lower endpoint
\(X_{\min}=A-Y_{\max}\).  The convex combination
\[
 X_{\mathrm{num}}
 =\frac{12}{25}X_{\min}+\frac{13}{25}X_{\max}             \tag{3.2}
\]
was selected because it lies well inside the numerical feasible
interval.

This numerical construction is not used as proof.  Let
\(\operatorname{Rat}_{10^8}\) mean: round the upper-triangular real
and imaginary entries to denominator \(10^8\), make the diagonal
real, and fill the lower triangle by Hermitian conjugation.  The
accepted matrix is the exact rational matrix
\[
 X^\#:=\operatorname{Rat}_{10^8}(X_{\mathrm{num}}).       \tag{3.3}
\]
After (3.3), all products, congruences, absolute-value bounds, and
sign decisions below use integer or rational arithmetic.

\begin{lemma}[Exact rational congruence floor]
\label{lem:newv3-congruence}
Let \(M=M^*\in M_n(\mathbb C)\), and let \(S\) be invertible.  Suppose
exact bounds give
\[
 S^*MS\succeq\beta I,\qquad
 \|S^*S-I\|_{\mathrm{op}}\le\varepsilon<1.                \tag{3.4}
\]
Then
\[
 M\succeq\frac{\beta}{1+\varepsilon}I.                   \tag{3.5}
\]
It suffices in (3.4) to replace the operator norm by an exact
Frobenius-norm upper bound.
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
The second inequality implies
\(S^*S\preceq(1+\varepsilon)I\), and \(\varepsilon<1\)
implies that \(S\) is invertible.  Given \(x=Sy\),
\[
 x^*Mx=y^*S^*MSy\ge\beta\|y\|^2
 \ge\frac{\beta}{1+\varepsilon}\|Sy\|^2.
\]
The Frobenius norm dominates the operator norm.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Exact full active-circle certificates]
\label{thm:newv3-circles}
For both signs \(\sigma\), the covariant pencils satisfy the
following inequalities for every \(k_1\in\mathbb R\):
\[
\begin{aligned}
 P_{\mathrm W,\sigma}
 (e^{ik_1};\pi,\pi,0)
 &\succeq
 \frac{914426469665866899773}
 {187500086341125000000000}\,I_{45},\\
 P_{\mathrm E,\sigma}
 (e^{ik_1};\pi,0,0)
 &\succeq
 \frac{53855251091745926193469}
 {300000135408300000000000}\,I_{45}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.6}
\]
The lower bounds are approximately \(0.004876938925789\) and
\(0.179517422612\), respectively.  Thus the two transverse anchors
are certified on their entire active \(k_1\)-circles, not merely at
sampled \(k_1\)-values.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
At each stated transverse corner and for \(\sigma=1\), form the exact
coefficient pair \(A,B\) of (2.3), the rational splitting (3.3), and
\[
 {\cal Q}^{\#}=
 \begin{pmatrix}A-X^\#&B\\B^*&X^\#\end{pmatrix}.          \tag{3.7}
\]
Hermitian symmetry and Laurent adjoint pairing are checked
coefficient by coefficient.

For Wilson, an exactly rounded approximate eigenbasis \(S\), exact
congruence, rowwise rational square-root ceilings, and
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv3-congruence} give
\[
 {\cal Q}^{\#}_{\mathrm W}\succeq
 \eta_{\mathrm W}I_{90},\qquad
 \eta_{\mathrm W}=
 \frac{914426469665866899773}
 {375000172682250000000000}>0,                            \tag{3.8}
\]
with
\[
 \|S^*S-I\|_{\mathrm F}\le
 \frac{230243}{500000000000}.                             \tag{3.9}
\]
For the endpoint the same exact calculation gives
\[
 {\cal Q}^{\#}_{\mathrm E}\succeq
 \eta_{\mathrm E}I_{90},\qquad
 \eta_{\mathrm E}=
 \frac{53855251091745926193469}
 {600000270816600000000000}>0,                            \tag{3.10}
\]
with
\[
 \|S^*S-I\|_{\mathrm F}\le
 \frac{451361}{1000000000000}.                            \tag{3.11}
\]
For \(w_z=(u,zu)\), equation (2.9) at \(\delta=0\) and
\(\|w_z\|^2=2\|u\|^2\) imply
\[
 u^*P(z)u=w_z^*{\cal Q}^{\#}w_z
 \ge2\eta_e\|u\|^2.                                      \tag{3.12}
\]
This is exactly (3.6).  At the declared transverse corners, changing
\(\sigma\) complex-conjugates \(A,B,X^\#\); hence conjugating (3.7)
gives the certificate for \(\sigma=-1\).

The upper-triangular rational splittings are bound to the calculation
by
\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
\mathrm W&
\texttt{9A3BFF3A55BFA4D3D4F0BBEC02736470DA40207E2288B916B85204A1A81B3735}\\
\mathrm E&
\texttt{42EE8F6C7783472D0CD9C4591299453EFB2BFFF7921FE5DFA7BDB3FCBAB135A9}.
\end{array}                                               \tag{3.13}
\]
The canonical payload SHA--256 of the complete recomputation is
\[
\texttt{381922A0FFA82A71585B7AB50C39095A554EAD10A4BFAC5972007D56FA1508C2}.
                                                               \tag{3.14}
\]
It is produced by
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v3\_corner\_block\_splitting.py}.
\]
The floating Riccati stage can only propose (3.3); failure of any
exact check aborts the theorem record.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[The midpoint failure was ansatz-specific]
\label{cor:newv3-midpoint}
At both transverse corners of
Countertheorem~\ref{ctr:newv2-midpoint}, the direct covariant pencil
is strictly positive on the complete active circle even though every
scalar splitting \(X=tA\) fails.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Combine Countertheorem~\ref{ctr:newv2-midpoint},
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv2-midpoint-optimal}, and
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv3-circles}.
\end{proof}

\begin{decision}[V4 transports the matrix splitting transversely]
\label{dec:newv3-next}
The active-coordinate elimination is now constructive.  Extend the
exact coefficient grouper from transverse signs to the full
quarter-turn phases \(\{1,i,-1,-i\}^3\).  At every transverse
quarter-turn anchor, construct and rationally certify a
noncommutative splitting.  Use covariance to identify duplicate
orbits before running expensive exact congruences.

For each certified anchor \(\vartheta_0\), keep \(X^\#(\vartheta_0)\)
fixed and bound
\[
 \left\|{\cal Q}_{X^\#}(\vartheta)
       -{\cal Q}_{X^\#}(\vartheta_0)\right\|_{\mathrm{op}} \tag{3.15}
\]
by exact transverse Laurent coefficient stocks.  This produces an
honest three-dimensional neighbourhood.  If the neighbourhoods do
not cover the transverse torus, introduce face or interior anchors
adaptively; do not replace the matrix splitting by the already
refuted scalar ansatz.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V3]
\label{ass:newv3-progress}
The noncommutative splitting sought in V2 exists at the two
previously obstructive transverse corners and has been converted
into an exact certificate.  One continuous momentum coordinate is
therefore eliminated on two complete circles.  The remaining
finite-dimensional problem is now a three-variable transport and
cover problem for explicit rational block matrices.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V3]
\label{fire:newv3-final}
The two active circles do not cover the transverse three-torus.
No global covariant Loewner inequality, continuum response sign,
interacting Yang--Mills measure, reflection-positive regulator
removal, exponential clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap follows
from Theorem~\ref{thm:newv3-circles} alone.
\end{firewall}

\section{V4: exact block certificates on the transverse quarter-turn grid}
\label{sec:newv4-grid}

\subsection{Exact extension of the coefficient grouper}

Write a transverse quarter-turn point as
\[
 m=(m_2,m_3,m_4)\in\{0,1,2,3\}^3,\qquad
 \vartheta_m=\frac{\pi}{2}m.                              \tag{4.1}
\]
For a Laurent exponent \(\alpha\), its transverse phase is exactly
\[
 i^{\alpha_2m_2+\alpha_3m_3+\alpha_4m_4}\in\{1,i,-1,-i\}. \tag{4.2}
\]
Thus the grouping in Proposition~\ref{prop:newv2-degree} remains
Gaussian-rational at all \(64\) anchors.  For each action and each
anchor, V4 constructs the \(\sigma=1\) splitting by the V3
Riccati-selection/rational-acceptance pipeline.  This is \(128\)
direct \(90\times90\) exact positivity checks.

\begin{lemma}[Sign recovery by momentum inversion]
\label{lem:newv4-inversion}
For the exact reduced Hessians and the covariant derivative (2.2),
\[
 H_e(-k)=\overline{H_e(k)},\qquad
 \nabla_1H_e(-k)=-\overline{\nabla_1H_e(k)}.              \tag{4.3}
\]
Consequently
\[
 P_{e,-}(-k)=\overline{P_{e,+}(k)}.                       \tag{4.4}
\]
Therefore plus-sign certificates at every transverse grid point
imply minus-sign certificates at every transverse grid point.
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
The Laurent coefficients of \(H_e\) are real and satisfy the
Hermitian adjoint pairing, so the first identity follows termwise.
The coefficient of the covariant derivative at exponent \(\alpha\)
is the coefficient of \(H_e\) multiplied by
\[
 i\left(\alpha_1-\frac{s_{i,1}-s_{j,1}}2\right).
\]
Complex conjugation changes the sign of this purely imaginary
factor, proving the second identity.  Equation (4.4) follows from
the definition \(P_{e,\sigma}=2H_e+\sigma\nabla_1H_e\).
The map \(m\mapsto-m\pmod4\) permutes the grid, and
\(k_1\mapsto-k_1\) permutes the active circle.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Exact transverse-grid active-circle gate]
\label{thm:newv4-grid}
For every transverse quarter-turn anchor \(m\), every
\(k_1\in\mathbb R\), and both signs \(\sigma\),
\[
\begin{aligned}
 P_{\mathrm W,\sigma}(e^{ik_1};\vartheta_m)
 &\succeq
 \frac{914426469665866899773}
 {187500086341125000000000}\,I_{45},\\
 P_{\mathrm E,\sigma}(e^{ik_1};\vartheta_m)
 &\succeq
 \frac{15922160354703546115783}
 {125000046961875000000000}\,I_{45}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{4.5}
\]
The uniform lower floors are approximately
\[
 0.004876938925789\quad\text{and}\quad0.1273772349826.    \tag{4.6}
\]
Thus all four momentum coordinates are treated continuously in
\(k_1\) and exactly on the \(4^3\) transverse grid.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
For each action and each \(m\), group the exact coefficients by
active exponent using (4.2), construct the rational Hermitian
splitting \(X_m^\#\) with denominator \(10^8\), and form
\[
 {\cal Q}_{e,m}^{\#}
 =
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A_{e,+}(\vartheta_m)-X_m^\#&B_{e,+}(\vartheta_m)\\
 B_{e,+}(\vartheta_m)^*&X_m^\#
 \end{pmatrix}.                                           \tag{4.7}
\]
Every Laurent adjoint identity and Hermitian identity is checked
exactly.  Lemma~\ref{lem:newv3-congruence}, applied to an exact
rational approximate eigenbasis for each block, proves all \(128\)
matrices positive definite.

The smallest Wilson block floor occurs at \(m=(2,2,0)\) and is
\[
 \eta_{\mathrm W}=
 \frac{914426469665866899773}
 {375000172682250000000000}.                              \tag{4.8}
\]
The smallest endpoint block floor occurs at \(m=(2,2,2)\) and is
\[
 \eta_{\mathrm E}=
 \frac{15922160354703546115783}
 {250000093923750000000000}.                              \tag{4.9}
\]
The largest exact upper bounds for the eigenbasis Gram errors over
the corresponding \(64\) blocks are
\[
 \frac{21013}{40000000000},\qquad
 \frac{131053}{250000000000},                             \tag{4.10}
\]
both below one.  Equation (3.12) doubles the block floors, giving
(4.5) for \(\sigma=1\); Lemma~\ref{lem:newv4-inversion} gives
\(\sigma=-1\).

The sorted splitting/floor ledgers have SHA--256 values
\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
\mathrm W&
\texttt{4850EF23E1B9299D0D55D445B0A64DA888A6497663DDB8A459EE1F0F23ECE95E}\\
\mathrm E&
\texttt{546A5DAEB2F6B525F8FAFF19112B60BE0B3E40C9FAD8F33C7139D23C21F66883}.
\end{array}                                               \tag{4.11}
\]
The exact verifier is
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v4\_transverse\_quarter\_block\_grid.py},
\]
and its canonical payload SHA--256 is
\[
\texttt{CCE3AEEF7462EB1B58508C368D1C9AAB453AC84A044369C36EB7E503C83CFD1A}.
                                                               \tag{4.12}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[The new grid is stronger than V99]
\label{rem:newv4-strength}
V99 checked \(2H\pm\nabla_1H\) at \(4^4=256\) isolated momenta.
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv4-grid} checks the same transverse anchors but
all \(k_1\) on each associated circle.  It therefore closes the
interpolation problem in one coordinate rather than merely refining
the old point grid.
\end{remark}

\begin{decision}[V5 performs the scheduled audit and transverse transport test]
\label{dec:newv4-next}
Because V5 is a multiple of five, first inspect the current
Standard-Model--Einstein and Navier--Stokes notes and import only
compatible, proved devices.  Then freeze each exact
\(X_m^\#\) and calculate exact first- and higher-order transverse
Laurent stocks for (4.7).  Compare the resulting certified
neighbourhoods with the three-dimensional quarter-turn Voronoi
cells.

If the fixed-anchor neighbourhoods fail to cover, do not conclude
that the pencil fails.  Refine the transverse grid and recompute
matrix splittings at new anchors, or construct a locally varying
splitting.  Retain an exact uncovered-point ledger so the refinement
is adaptive rather than uniform by guesswork.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V4]
\label{ass:newv4-progress}
The exact noncommutative certificate now covers \(64\) complete
momentum circles for each action and sign.  This is the first
certificate in the programme that removes one torus coordinate
globally while retaining the difficult covariant derivative and
constraint-compressed \(45\)-dimensional fibre.  The remaining
finite problem is a transverse three-torus cover.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V4]
\label{fire:newv4-final}
The transverse quarter-turn grid has not yet been thickened into a
cover of \(\mathbb T^3\).  Hence no full-torus covariant Loewner
inequality, continuum response sign, interacting Yang--Mills
measure, reflection-positive regulator removal, exponential
clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap has yet been proved.
\end{firewall}

\section{V5: companion audit and an exact fixed-splitting transport no-go}
\label{sec:newv5-audit-transport}

\subsection{Required five-version companion audit}

\begin{audit}[Current companion files]
\label{aud:newv5-files}
The current files inspected before choosing the V5 direction are
\[
\begin{array}{c|r|r|l}
\text{programme}&\text{version}&\text{bytes}&\text{SHA--256}\\ \hline
\text{SM--Einstein}&V16&205772&
\texttt{BFA97971E5037A26F713B7B1CEBF66C343517EC188E860B8DC4C6097AE9EC06D}\\
\text{Navier--Stokes}&V26&278283&
\texttt{82C887F8C2C69E4F28FAD7C3DC8DE5B9099CB5A4BF431EBA1FFF3E91935978AD}.
\end{array}                                                \tag{5.1}
\]
Their source line counts are \(6331\) and \(5319\), respectively.
The SM programme has advanced from V2 at the preceding YM V100 audit
to V16; the NS file is unchanged.
\end{audit}

The relevant new SM V16 results are:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=3.3em]
\item positivity of \(J^*J+\Pi\), where \(J\Pi=0\) and \(\Pi\) is a
declared kernel projector, proves that no hidden kernel remains;
\item the same one-coordinate matrix block certificate used here
propagates the completed incidence floor; and
\item a finite-cover proof must subtract a certified block variation
from each anchor floor, with adaptive refinement where the margin is
small.
\end{enumerate}
Items (ii)--(iii) confirm the matrix-valued finite-cover direction
selected in V4.  Item (i) is retained for the later YM gauge-kernel
landing, but it does not change the present reduced-fibre calculation.
No SM numerical constant is imported.

NS V26 computes exact norms of first and second Oseen-kernel
derivatives on rank-one input tensors.  It proves a useful
methodological restriction: an ambient operator norm may be replaced
by a smaller structured norm only after proving that the actual
perturbation lies in that structured class.  The present transverse
block variation is not rank one, so importing the NS rank-one
constants would be invalid.  The audit therefore recommends first
using the actual Fourier support of the moving splitting, not an
unrelated restricted norm.

\subsection{Exact fixed-\(X\) transverse stock}

Fix an action, a plus-sign transverse anchor \(\vartheta_m\), and its
exact rational splitting \(X_m^\#\).  Keep \(X_m^\#\) fixed away from
the anchor and write
\[
 {\cal Q}_{m}(\vartheta)
 =
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A(\vartheta)-X_m^\#&B(\vartheta)\\
 B(\vartheta)^*&X_m^\#
 \end{pmatrix}.                                           \tag{5.2}
\]
Let its varying part have transverse Fourier coefficients
\[
 {\cal Q}_{m}(\vartheta)-{\cal Q}_{m}(\vartheta_m)
 =\sum_{\beta\in\mathbb Z^3}
 C_\beta\left(e^{i\beta\cdot\vartheta}
             -e^{i\beta\cdot\vartheta_m}\right).          \tag{5.3}
\]
The coefficients \(C_\beta\) are independent of \(m\), because the
fixed \(X_m^\#\) cancels from the difference.

\begin{lemma}[Exact Fourier--Frobenius transport bound]
\label{lem:newv5-stock}
Define
\[
 L_j:=\sum_\beta|\beta_j|\,\widehat{\|C_\beta\|_{\mathrm F}},
 \qquad j=2,3,4,                                          \tag{5.4}
\]
where every hat is a rational upper bound whose square is checked
against the exact Gaussian-rational coefficient norm.  Then
\[
 \|{\cal Q}_{m}(\vartheta)-{\cal Q}_{m}(\vartheta_m)\|_{\rm op}
 \le\sum_{j=2}^4 L_j|\vartheta_j-\vartheta_{m,j}|.         \tag{5.5}
\]
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
For real \(a,b\),
\[
 |e^{ia}-e^{ib}|\le |a-b|.
\]
Apply this termwise to (5.3), use a coordinatewise path,
\(\|\cdot\|_{\rm op}\le\|\cdot\|_{\rm F}\), and the triangle
inequality.  All replacements of square roots by hats enlarge the
right side.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[The global fixed-splitting stock cannot cover a quarter cell]
\label{thm:newv5-no-cover}
The exact stocks in Lemma~\ref{lem:newv5-stock} are
\[
\begin{array}{c|c|c|c}
e&L_2&L_3&L_4\\ \hline
\mathrm W&
3159902107633/250000000000&
3159902107633/250000000000&
3159902107633/250000000000\\
\mathrm E&
65268517493039/125000000000&
262302017752613/500000000000&
131401686144179/250000000000 .
\end{array}                                                \tag{5.6}
\]
Even using the \emph{largest} exact V4 anchor block floor, the
largest isotropic half-side certified by (5.5) is
\[
\begin{aligned}
h_{\mathrm W}^{\max}
&=
\frac{4011859086859741683979}
{16250931544117640499001368}
<\frac1{4000},\\
h_{\mathrm E}^{\max}
&=
\frac{504807300447604871241289}
{4717079035610742292275025086}
<\frac1{9000}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{5.7}
\]
Since a transverse quarter-turn Voronoi cell has coordinate
half-side \(\pi/4>3/4\), the global Frobenius stock with \(X_m^\#\)
held fixed cannot certify any whole quarter cell, for either action.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The varying \(90\times90\) coefficient at frequency \(\beta\) has
top-left block \(A_\beta\), top-right block \(B_\beta\),
bottom-left block \(B_{-\beta}^*\), and zero bottom-right block.
Therefore
\[
 \|C_\beta\|_{\mathrm F}^2
 =\|A_\beta\|_{\mathrm F}^2
  +\|B_\beta\|_{\mathrm F}^2
  +\|B_{-\beta}\|_{\mathrm F}^2.                          \tag{5.8}
\]
Exact coefficient collection gives thirteen nonzero transverse
frequencies for Wilson and twenty-five for the endpoint.  Rational
square-root ceilings in (5.8), followed by (5.4), give (5.6).

If an anchor block has floor \(\rho_m\), (5.5) certifies an isotropic
box of half-side at most
\[
 h_m=\frac{\rho_m}{L_2+L_3+L_4}.                          \tag{5.9}
\]
All \(128\) anchor floors were recomputed exactly.  Substitution of
their maxima into (5.9) gives (5.7), including the exact rational
comparisons with \(1/4000\) and \(1/9000\).  These numbers are
strictly smaller than \(3/4<\pi/4\).

The exact verifier is
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v5\_transverse\_transport\_stock.py},
\]
with canonical payload SHA--256
\[
\texttt{98B4F722A5F88F208D8A13CBC7E3A97078739E4E96145E4D17EA4964F8BB0744}.
                                                               \tag{5.10}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[What the no-cover theorem says]
\label{fire:newv5-no-cover}
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv5-no-cover} refutes only a coarse proof device:
freezing \(X\) and using a global coefficientwise Frobenius stock.
It neither produces an uncovered negative pencil nor refutes the
existence of a varying matrix splitting.  The very small radii arise
from discarding cancellations between the moving \(A\), \(B\), and
optimal \(X\).
\end{firewall}

\begin{decision}[V6 constructs a structured moving splitting]
\label{dec:newv5-next}
Use the \(4^3\) exact rational splittings as samples of a Hermitian
matrix field.  Form a tensor-product real trigonometric cardinal
interpolant \(X_{\mathrm{int}}(\vartheta)\) which agrees with
\(X_m^\#\) at every quarter-turn anchor.  Test its full block
\[
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A(\vartheta)-X_{\mathrm{int}}(\vartheta)&B(\vartheta)\\
 B(\vartheta)^*&X_{\mathrm{int}}(\vartheta)
 \end{pmatrix}                                            \tag{5.11}
\]
on dense deterministic and random transverse sets.  If it remains
positive, compile its exact Laurent coefficients and seek local
interval/congruence boxes.  If it fails, retain an exact negative
witness and move upstream to locally recomputed Riccati splittings
rather than increasing a uniform grid blindly.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V5]
\label{ass:newv5-progress}
The required companion audit has been completed.  The SM programme
supports the matrix-valued block/finite-cover architecture; the NS
programme prevents an unjustified structured-norm shortcut.  The
first transverse thickening attempt has been closed exactly, and the
next object is a moving Hermitian splitting designed to preserve the
cancellations seen at all V4 anchors.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V5]
\label{fire:newv5-final}
No transverse cover, full-torus covariant Loewner inequality,
continuum response sign, interacting Yang--Mills measure,
reflection-positive regulator removal, exponential clustering, or
Hamiltonian mass gap has yet been proved.
\end{firewall}

\section{V6: an exact degree-one moving splitting and the recursive route}
\label{sec:newv6-degree-one}

\subsection{Removing the Nyquist modes}

The full four-sample real cardinal function
\[
 \ell_m(\theta)=\frac14\left[
 1+2\cos\left(\theta-\frac{m\pi}{2}\right)
 +\cos\left(2\theta-m\pi\right)\right]                    \tag{6.1}
\]
interpolates the V4 splitting samples exactly, but contains
frequencies \(\pm2\).  Floating mesh and random tests found its block
positive, with sampled minima about \(0.002426\) for Wilson and
\(0.063699\) for the endpoint.  To preserve the possibility of
recursive coordinate elimination, V6 deletes the Nyquist term and
uses
\[
 \ell_m^{(1)}(\theta)
 =\frac14\left[
 1+2\cos\left(\theta-\frac{m\pi}{2}\right)\right].         \tag{6.2}
\]
These weights are real and have Laurent support \(\{-1,0,1\}\).
They are no longer cardinal; positivity must therefore be checked
again rather than inherited from V4.

For the \(64\) exact rational plus-sign splittings
\(X_m^\#\), define
\[
 X_e^{(1)}(\vartheta)
 :=
 \sum_{m\in\{0,1,2,3\}^3}
 \prod_{j=2}^4\ell_{m_j}^{(1)}(\vartheta_j)\,X_{e,m}^\#.
                                                               \tag{6.3}
\]

\begin{proposition}[Exact structure of the truncated splitting]
\label{prop:newv6-structure}
The field \(X_e^{(1)}\) is Hermitian for every real
\(\vartheta\), has Gaussian-rational Laurent coefficients, and has
degree at most one in each transverse phase.  Hence
\[
 {\cal R}_e(\vartheta):=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A_{e,+}(\vartheta)-X_e^{(1)}(\vartheta)&
 B_{e,+}(\vartheta)\\
 B_{e,+}(\vartheta)^*&X_e^{(1)}(\vartheta)
 \end{pmatrix}                                            \tag{6.4}
\]
is a Hermitian \(90\times90\) Laurent polynomial of degree at most
one in each of \(k_2,k_3,k_4\).
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
Each \(X_{e,m}^\#\) is Hermitian and each scalar weight in (6.3) is
real, proving pointwise Hermiticity.  Expanding
\[
 \ell_m^{(1)}(\theta)
 =\frac14+\frac14i^{-m}e^{i\theta}
  +\frac14i^m e^{-i\theta}                                \tag{6.5}
\]
proves the Gaussian-rational coefficient claim and the coordinate
degree bound.  The original \(A\) and \(B\) have coordinate degree
one, so the same statements hold for (6.4).  Adjoint pairing follows
from pointwise Hermiticity, or directly from (6.5).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Exact grid gate for the degree-one moving block]
\label{thm:newv6-grid}
At every transverse quarter-turn point
\(\vartheta_m\), the exact block (6.4) is positive definite.  Its
uniform floors obey
\[
\begin{aligned}
 {\cal R}_{\mathrm W}(\vartheta_m)
 &\succeq
 \frac{68828425658523634599039}
 {32000015949920000000000000}\,I_{90},\\
 {\cal R}_{\mathrm E}(\vartheta_m)
 &\succeq
 \frac{926243137535429492157601}
 {14769238358976000000000000}\,I_{90}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{6.6}
\]
Consequently, for all \(k_1\), all transverse quarter-turn
\(\vartheta_m\), and both signs,
\[
\begin{aligned}
 P_{\mathrm W,\sigma}(e^{ik_1};\vartheta_m)
 &\succeq
 \frac{68828425658523634599039}
 {16000007974960000000000000}\,I_{45},\\
 P_{\mathrm E,\sigma}(e^{ik_1};\vartheta_m)
 &\succeq
 \frac{926243137535429492157601}
 {7384619179488000000000000}\,I_{45}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{6.7}
\]
The block floors are approximately \(0.002150887229751\) and
\(0.06271434687575\).
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
At a quarter-turn target \(n\), (6.2) takes the exact rational
values
\[
 \ell_m^{(1)}(n\pi/2)=
 \begin{cases}
 3/4,&n-m\equiv0\pmod4,\\
 1/4,&n-m\equiv1,3\pmod4,\\
 -1/4,&n-m\equiv2\pmod4.
 \end{cases}                                              \tag{6.8}
\]
Thus every entry of (6.3)--(6.4) is Gaussian-rational at all
\(64\) targets.  Exact rational congruence and
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv3-congruence} certify all \(128\) action/target
blocks.  The worst points are \(m=(2,2,0)\) for Wilson and
\(m=(2,2,2)\) for the endpoint, and their exact floors are those in
(6.6).  The largest exact Gram-error upper bounds in the rounded
congruence bases are
\[
 \frac{525721}{10^{12}},\qquad
 \frac{52679}{10^{11}},                                   \tag{6.9}
\]
both below one.  Apply (2.9) with \(\delta=0\) to double the block
floors and obtain (6.7) for the plus pencil.
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv4-inversion} supplies the minus pencil on the
inversion-invariant grid.

The sorted exact certificate ledgers have SHA--256 values
\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
\mathrm W&
\texttt{6F78BD31A81885141BEA8C833EA2579B7BCCB57CCE3BC8B9399443E83155133D}\\
\mathrm E&
\texttt{F9555BB8BCBEAE55CC7A75C099229F9E33D5BFEB1651081B519AF791ED713EDA}.
\end{array}                                               \tag{6.10}
\]
The verifier
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v6\_degree\_one\_block\_grid.py}
\]
has canonical payload SHA--256
\[
\texttt{F95229217A29CB7ECDE2A8B3B86052066C3AA7962EA5C87B1DEE682CFF510FC7}.
                                                               \tag{6.11}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{record}[Global route-selection evidence]
\label{rec:newv6-diagnostic}
On a \(7^3\) mesh plus \(1024\) seeded random transverse points, the
degree-one block (6.4) had sampled minima
\[
 0.0021559770903\quad(\mathrm W),\qquad
 0.0618315020802\quad(\mathrm E),                          \tag{6.12}
\]
and neither principal diagonal block lost positivity.  The
diagnostic payload SHA--256 is
\[
\texttt{9C2FA3509F4BD24AC5E472D20A32A8F1652265BBAE9F9972AAB407E43C007F82}.
                                                               \tag{6.13}
\]
This record selects the next proof route but is not used as a
positivity certificate away from the exact grid.
\end{record}

\begin{decision}[V7 eliminates the second momentum coordinate]
\label{dec:newv6-next}
Use Proposition~\ref{prop:newv6-structure} to regroup (6.4) as
\[
 {\cal R}_e(k_2;k_3,k_4)
 =A_e^{(2)}(k_3,k_4)
  +e^{ik_2}B_e^{(2)}(k_3,k_4)
  +e^{-ik_2}B_e^{(2)}(k_3,k_4)^*.                         \tag{6.14}
\]
At the \(4^2\) exact \((k_3,k_4)\) anchors, construct rational
noncommutative splittings for the resulting \(180\times180\) block.
If all succeed, build a degree-one two-variable field from those
samples and repeat for \(k_3\) and \(k_4\).  This recursive route
replaces a three-dimensional small-box cover by three finite
one-coordinate eliminations.

If a higher-dimensional splitting fails, retain an exact witness
and first test whether only the degree-one truncation is responsible;
the positive full-cardinal interpolant remains an available
higher-degree fallback.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V6]
\label{ass:newv6-progress}
The moving splitting has been compressed to degree one without
losing any exact quarter-grid gate.  This creates a finite recursive
certificate architecture: dimensions \(45\), \(90\), \(180\),
\(360\), and \(720\) successively remove the four torus coordinates.
Only the first elimination and the grid input to the second are
proved so far.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V6]
\label{fire:newv6-final}
The diagnostic positivity of (6.4) between transverse anchors is
not a proof.  No second-coordinate block certificate, full-torus
covariant Loewner inequality, continuum response sign, interacting
Yang--Mills measure, reflection-positive regulator removal,
exponential clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap has yet been proved.
\end{firewall}

\section{V7: exact recursive elimination of the second coordinate}
\label{sec:newv7-second}

\subsection{Exact regrouping of the V6 block}

By Proposition~\ref{prop:newv6-structure}, the plus-sign block
\({\cal R}_e\) has an exact Laurent expansion
\[
 {\cal R}_e(k_2,k_3,k_4)
 =\sum_{\beta\in\{-1,0,1\}^3}
 R_{e,\beta}e^{i(\beta_2k_2+\beta_3k_3+\beta_4k_4)},
 \qquad R_{e,-\beta}=R_{e,\beta}^*.                       \tag{7.1}
\]
The splitting part of \(R_{e,\beta}\) is obtained directly from
(6.3)--(6.5):
\[
 \widehat X_{e,\beta}
 =\frac1{64}\sum_{m\in\{0,1,2,3\}^3}
 i^{-(\beta_2m_2+\beta_3m_3+\beta_4m_4)}X_{e,m}^\#.
                                                               \tag{7.2}
\]
Thus every coefficient in (7.1) is Gaussian-rational.

For \(q=(q_3,q_4)\in\{0,1,2,3\}^2\), set
\((k_3,k_4)=\pi q/2\), and regroup (7.1) in \(z_2=e^{ik_2}\):
\[
 {\cal R}_{e,q}(z_2)
 =A_{e,q}^{(2)}+z_2B_{e,q}^{(2)}
  +\bar z_2B_{e,q}^{(2)*}.                                \tag{7.3}
\]
All entries in (7.3) remain Gaussian-rational.

\begin{theorem}[Exact \(k_2\) block gate on the remaining grid]
\label{thm:newv7-second}
For every action and all \(16\) remaining quarter-turn anchors
\(q\), there is an exact Hermitian rational
\(Y_{e,q}^\#\in M_{90}(\mathbb C)\) such that
\[
 {\cal S}_{e,q}:=
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A_{e,q}^{(2)}-Y_{e,q}^\#&B_{e,q}^{(2)}\\
 B_{e,q}^{(2)*}&Y_{e,q}^\#
 \end{pmatrix}\succ0.                                    \tag{7.4}
\]
Uniform exact floors are
\[
\begin{aligned}
 {\cal S}_{\mathrm W,q}
 &\succeq
 \frac{40818212122355598206339387}
 {1600000016552000000000000000000}\,I_{180},\\
 {\cal S}_{\mathrm E,q}
 &\succeq
 \frac{6690252808082327544286220567}
 {8000000082912000000000000000000}\,I_{180}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{7.5}
\]
Consequently, for all \(k_1,k_2\), every remaining grid anchor
\((k_3,k_4)=\pi q/2\), and both signs,
\[
\begin{aligned}
 P_{\mathrm W,\sigma}(k)
 &\succeq
 \frac{40818212122355598206339387}
 {400000004138000000000000000000}\,I_{45},\\
 P_{\mathrm E,\sigma}(k)
 &\succeq
 \frac{6690252808082327544286220567}
 {2000000020728000000000000000000}\,I_{45}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{7.6}
\]
The original-pencil floors are approximately
\(0.0001020455292502\) and \(0.003345126369372\).
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
At every \(q\), exact phase collection in (7.1) proves the adjoint
pairing in (7.3).  Floating Riccati iteration supplies only a
candidate interval of splittings.  Take the \(13/25\) convex point,
round its Hermitian upper triangle to denominator \(10^{10}\), and
call the resulting exact matrix \(Y_{e,q}^\#\).  Form (7.4) using
exact arithmetic.

For each of the \(32\) action/anchor matrices, round a numerical
eigenbasis to denominator \(10^{10}\), compute its congruence with
(7.4) exactly, and apply
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv3-congruence}.  Every rational Gershgorin floor is
positive.  The worst Wilson anchor is \(q=(2,0)\), where the first
fraction in (7.5) is attained.  The worst endpoint anchor is
\(q=(1,2)\), where the second fraction is attained.  The largest
exact Gram-error upper bounds are
\[
 \frac{10443}{10^{12}},\qquad \frac{10441}{10^{12}}.      \tag{7.7}
\]

For \(v_{z_2}=(w,z_2w)\), Theorem~\ref{thm:newv2-block}
gives
\[
 w^*{\cal R}_{e,q}(z_2)w
 =v_{z_2}^*{\cal S}_{e,q}v_{z_2}
 \ge2\eta_{e,q}\|w\|^2.                                  \tag{7.8}
\]
With \(w=(u,z_1u)\), the first elimination gives
\[
 u^*P_{e,+}(k)u
 =w^*{\cal R}_{e,q}(z_2)w
 \ge4\eta_{e,q}\|u\|^2,                                  \tag{7.9}
\]
which is (7.6) for the plus sign.  Momentum inversion gives the
minus sign on the inversion-invariant \(q\)-grid.

The sorted \(Y^\#\)/floor ledgers have SHA--256 values
\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
\mathrm W&
\texttt{6C3DEE9C4CE9B9DDE7022A1A05DE71F4C4102642132E6550577261E48C8D18C8}\\
\mathrm E&
\texttt{AA76F0A8B1A5AA438AF2810E5853C0575F56182E36ED568D2DAA13F3145941FF}.
\end{array}                                               \tag{7.10}
\]
The exact verifier
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v7\_second\_coordinate\_block\_grid.py}
\]
has canonical payload SHA--256
\[
\texttt{00140D5F91202BC70C97734637AB93F34B6A2F10987483FA0EAFB2DB78994447}.
                                                               \tag{7.11}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Precision was load-bearing]
\label{rem:newv7-precision}
The smallest Wilson \(180\times180\) block floor is only about
\(2.55114\times10^{-5}\).  V7 therefore increases both the splitting
and eigenbasis denominators from \(10^8\) to \(10^{10}\).  The exact
acceptance stage, rather than the floating Riccati residual, proves
the sign.
\end{remark}

\begin{decision}[V8 compresses the second splitting to degree one]
\label{dec:newv7-next}
From the \(4^2\) matrices \(Y_{e,q}^\#\), form
\[
 Y_e^{(1)}(k_3,k_4)
 =\sum_{q\in\{0,1,2,3\}^2}
 \ell_{q_3}^{(1)}(k_3)\ell_{q_4}^{(1)}(k_4)Y_{e,q}^\#.
                                                               \tag{7.12}
\]
This is Hermitian and degree one in each remaining phase.  Rebuild
the \(180\times180\) block (7.4) with \(Y_e^{(1)}\), certify it on
the \(4^2\) grid, and then regroup in \(k_3\).  The next certificate
has dimension \(360\) and only four remaining \(k_4\)-anchors.

If the degree-one truncation loses the small Wilson margin, first
optimize the convex Riccati weight locally; retain the full
four-sample cardinal field as the higher-degree fallback.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V7]
\label{ass:newv7-progress}
Two momentum coordinates have now been eliminated exactly at every
point of a \(4^2\) remaining grid.  The proof uses a finite hierarchy
of explicit rational block matrices, not a pointwise eigenvalue
scan.  Two recursive eliminations remain before a full-torus
covariant Loewner statement can be considered proved.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V7]
\label{fire:newv7-final}
The coordinates \(k_3,k_4\) are still discrete.  No full-torus
covariant Loewner inequality, continuum response sign, interacting
Yang--Mills measure, reflection-positive regulator removal,
exponential clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap has yet been proved.
\end{firewall}

\section{V8: exact failure of the naive second splitting interpolation}
\label{sec:newv8-no-go}

\subsection{The proposed degree-one field}

V7 supplies exact rational \(90\times90\) splittings
\(Y_{e,q}^\#\) at the sixteen remaining grid points.  The direct
analogue of V6 is
\[
 Y_e^{(1)}(k_3,k_4)
 =
 \sum_{q\in\{0,1,2,3\}^2}
 \ell_{q_3}^{(1)}(k_3)\ell_{q_4}^{(1)}(k_4)Y_{e,q}^\#,
                                                               \tag{8.1}
\]
where every \(Y_{e,q}^\#\) uses the same \(13/25\) position between
the two Riccati endpoints.  The associated candidate block is
\[
 {\cal S}_e^{(1)}(k_3,k_4)
 =
 \begin{pmatrix}
 A_e^{(2)}-Y_e^{(1)}&B_e^{(2)}\\
 B_e^{(2)*}&Y_e^{(1)}
 \end{pmatrix}.                                           \tag{8.2}
\]
It is Hermitian and has degree at most one in each remaining phase.
Unlike the full cardinal functions (6.1), the truncated functions
\(\ell^{(1)}\) do not interpolate the samples.  The V7 positivity
of each \(Y_{e,q}^\#\) therefore does not imply positivity of (8.2)
even at the same grid.

\begin{countertheorem}[Exact failure of the uniform-weight degree-one field]
\label{ctr:newv8-degree-one}
The block (8.2) is indefinite for both actions.  At
\[
 (k_3,k_4)=(3\pi/2,0)\quad\text{for Wilson},\qquad
 (k_3,k_4)=(\pi/2,\pi)\quad\text{for the endpoint},        \tag{8.3}
\]
there are Gaussian-integer vectors \(w_{\mathrm W}\) and
\(w_{\mathrm E}\) for which
\[
\begin{aligned}
 w_{\mathrm W}^*{\cal S}_{\mathrm W}^{(1)}w_{\mathrm W}
 &=-\frac{1472380938406827827563}{32000000000}<0,\\
 w_{\mathrm E}^*{\cal S}_{\mathrm E}^{(1)}w_{\mathrm E}
 &=-\frac{179739223510157736019}{3000000000}<0.
\end{aligned}
\tag{8.4}
\]
Thus the uniform-\(13/25\) tensor degree-one interpolation cannot
perform the third coordinate elimination.
\end{countertheorem}

\begin{proof}
Construct the exact V6 coefficient bank by (7.1)--(7.2), regroup it
as (7.3), and reconstruct all sixteen rational
\(Y_{e,q}^\#\) with denominator \(10^{10}\).  At the two points
(8.3), the weights (6.8) are rational.  Hence every entry of (8.2)
is Gaussian-rational.

Round a numerical negative eigenvector only to select an integer
candidate, discard zero components until further deletion would lose
the sign, and then recompute the quadratic form exactly.  The
resulting supports have \(156\) and \(124\) nonzero components out
of \(180\); their ordered Gaussian-integer ledgers have SHA--256
\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
\mathrm W&
\texttt{27FB5D25CDED3D71A08F4A5C58A7890B17C646F18C049A9ADEB4C66DAC7046A0}\\
\mathrm E&
\texttt{F82B4661C713DD74DD3109A554572B966F0B94EABC80C6E01830C64D29616889}.
\end{array}                                               \tag{8.5}
\]
Exact substitution gives (8.4).  The verifier
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v8\_degree\_one\_second\_counterexample.py}
\]
has canonical payload SHA--256
\[
\texttt{82F110C4F868507EA0DA305F261AA96386A7A0608C5A13785F69A0F5EF9C5459}.
                                                               \tag{8.6}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[Certificate failure, not pencil failure]
\label{fire:newv8-no-go}
The witnesses in Countertheorem~\ref{ctr:newv8-degree-one} are not
of the structured form required to produce a negative vector for
the underlying \({\cal R}_e(k_2;k_3,k_4)\), much less for
\(P_{e,\sigma}\).  They refute only the chosen interpolation of the
auxiliary V7 splittings.
\end{firewall}

\subsection{Upstream tests of the interpolation family}

Two further floating tests determine the next direction.
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=3.3em]
\item Allowing the sixteen Riccati weights to vary independently can
repair either displayed failure point: local optimized margins were
about \(2.48\cdot10^{-4}\) and \(4.21\cdot10^{-3}\).
However, those point-optimized weights create large negative blocks
elsewhere.  A common scalar weight cannot repair even the two
original failures simultaneously.
\item Restoring the full cardinal functions (6.1) makes the sampled
Wilson block positive, with minimum about \(5.99\cdot10^{-6}\), but
the sampled endpoint block still reaches about
\(-2.61\cdot10^{-4}\).
\end{enumerate}
These are diagnostic route records only.  They show that black-box
interpolation of separately optimized splittings is poorly
conditioned, even though a feasible splitting exists pointwise.

\begin{lemma}[Tangential factor identity]
\label{lem:newv8-tangent}
Let \(H={\cal A}^*{\cal A}\), let
\({\cal D}_1{\cal A}\) be the covariant factor derivative, and put
\[
 K={\cal A}^*{\cal D}_1{\cal A},\qquad
 \Pi_{\cal A}={\cal A}H^{-1}{\cal A}^*.                   \tag{8.7}
\]
Where \(H\succ0\),
\[
 \nabla_1H=K+K^*,\qquad
 ({\cal D}_1{\cal A})^*\Pi_{\cal A}
 ({\cal D}_1{\cal A})=K^*H^{-1}K.                        \tag{8.8}
\]
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
The first identity is the covariant product rule.  Substitute the
definition of \(\Pi_{\cal A}\) in the second and associate factors.
\end{proof}

The normal part \((I-\Pi_{\cal A}){\cal D}_1{\cal A}\) does not
enter \(\nabla_1H\).  Nevertheless, an upstream diagnostic found
sampled tangential-energy constants
\[
 \lambda_{\max}\!\left(
 H^{-1/2}K^*H^{-1}KH^{-1/2}\right)
 \approx1.7113\quad(\mathrm W),\qquad
 \approx1.6087\quad(\mathrm E),                            \tag{8.9}
\]
still above the unit threshold.  Thus merely projecting out the
normal derivative does not rescue the V2 Cauchy--Schwarz gate.
In contrast, the sampled norms of the Hermitian part
\[
 H^{-1/2}(K+K^*)H^{-1/2}                                  \tag{8.10}
\]
remain about \(1.2552\) and \(1.2488\), below \(2\).  The decisive
mechanism is cancellation of the \(H\)-skew tangential part, not an
energy contraction.

\begin{decision}[V9 isolates the skew-cancellation certificate]
\label{dec:newv8-next}
Abandon scalar energy majorants for \({\cal D}_1{\cal A}\).
Decompose the normalized mixed factor
\[
 C=H^{-1/2}KH^{-1/2}
\]
into Hermitian and skew-Hermitian parts.  Construct an explicit
\(H\)-skew column connection which removes the skew part without
changing \(\nabla_1H\), and express the direct covariant pencil as a
Gram/Schur certificate involving only
\((C+C^*)/2\).  Test whether this certificate admits exact
coefficient bounds smaller than one.

If the resulting estimate is merely a rewriting of the unknown
direct pencil, return to a shape-preserving piecewise interpolation
of the V7 splittings with certified local residuals.  Do not continue
unconstrained optimization of sixteen weights without a structural
invariant.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V8]
\label{ass:newv8-progress}
V8 closes the naive third-elimination interpolation by exact
counterexamples and identifies why two natural repairs are
insufficient.  The upstream factor analysis also separates the
normal derivative, the tangential energy, and the load-bearing
Hermitian cancellation.  The proven two-coordinate elimination of
V7 remains intact.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V8]
\label{fire:newv8-final}
No third-coordinate certificate or full-torus covariant Loewner
inequality has been proved.  Consequently no continuum response
sign, interacting Yang--Mills measure, reflection-positive regulator
removal, exponential clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap follows.
\end{firewall}

\section{V9: skew removal and the stronger four-thirds grid gate}
\label{sec:newv9-skew}

\subsection{The exact load-bearing factor}

Continue with the notation of Lemma~\ref{lem:newv8-tangent}.  Put
\[
 S:=\frac{K+K^*}{2},\qquad
 \Omega:=\frac12H^{-1}(K-K^*),\qquad
 {\cal B}:={\cal D}_1{\cal A}-{\cal A}\Omega.             \tag{9.1}
\]

\begin{theorem}[Exact skew-cancellation reduction]
\label{thm:newv9-skew}
Where \(H={\cal A}^*{\cal A}\succ0\), the operator \(\Omega\) is
\(H\)-skew:
\[
 \Omega^*H+H\Omega=0,                                    \tag{9.2}
\]
and the corrected derivative satisfies
\[
 {\cal A}^*{\cal B}=S=S^*,\qquad
 {\cal B}^*{\cal A}+{\cal A}^*{\cal B}=\nabla_1H.         \tag{9.3}
\]
Let \(\Pi_{\cal A}={\cal A}H^{-1}{\cal A}^*\).  For every \(q>0\),
the following statements are equivalent:
\[
\begin{array}{ll}
\textup{(i)}&2qH\pm\nabla_1H\succeq0\quad\text{for both signs},\\
\textup{(ii)}&-qH\preceq S\preceq qH,\\
\textup{(iii)}&S H^{-1}S\preceq q^2H,\\
\textup{(iv)}&
{\cal B}^*\Pi_{\cal A}{\cal B}\preceq q^2{\cal A}^*{\cal A}.
\end{array}                                               \tag{9.4}
\]
Thus the normal component and the \(H\)-skew tangential component of
the factor derivative are both irrelevant.  Only the projected
corrected derivative is load-bearing.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Since \(H\Omega=(K-K^*)/2\),
\[
 \Omega^*H=\frac{K^*-K}{2},
\]
which proves (9.2).  Moreover
\[
 {\cal A}^*{\cal B}=K-H\Omega
 =K-\frac{K-K^*}{2}=S.                                   \tag{9.5}
\]
Equation (9.2) also shows that subtracting
\({\cal A}\Omega\) does not change the covariant product rule, giving
the second identity in (9.3).

Normalize \(S\) by
\[
 T=H^{-1/2}SH^{-1/2}=T^*.                                \tag{9.6}
\]
Statements (i) and (ii) are both equivalent to
\(-qI\preceq T\preceq qI\).  By the spectral theorem this is
equivalent to \(T^2\preceq q^2I\), which after congruence by
\(H^{1/2}\) is (iii).  Finally, (9.5) gives
\[
 {\cal B}^*\Pi_{\cal A}{\cal B}
 =S H^{-1}S,                                              \tag{9.7}
\]
so (iii) and (iv) are identical.
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Why V2 failed]
\label{rem:newv9-v2}
The V2 gate bounded the full energy
\(({\cal D}_1{\cal A})^*{\cal D}_1{\cal A}\).  Theorem
\ref{thm:newv9-skew} first removes the normal component by
\(\Pi_{\cal A}\), then removes the \(H\)-skew component by
\(\Omega\).  The diagnostic constants in (8.9) show that the first
removal alone is insufficient.  Both removals are mathematically
forced.
\end{remark}

\subsection{The \(q=2/3\) certificate}

Choosing \(q=2/3\) in Theorem~\ref{thm:newv9-skew} gives the stronger
pair
\[
 \frac43H\pm\nabla_1H\succeq0.                            \tag{9.8}
\]
Equivalently, by a fixed unitary block diagonalization,
\[
 \begin{pmatrix}
 \frac23H&S\\S&\frac23H
 \end{pmatrix}\succeq0.                                  \tag{9.9}
\]
Unlike the full factor-energy inequality, (9.9) retains exactly the
cancellation seen in the direct normalized derivative.

\begin{theorem}[Exact four-thirds quarter-turn gate]
\label{thm:newv9-four-thirds}
At every \(k\in\{0,\pi/2,\pi,3\pi/2\}^4\), for both signs,
\[
\begin{aligned}
 \frac43H_{\mathrm W}(k)\pm\nabla_1H_{\mathrm W}(k)
 &\succeq
 \frac{269643785483}{38297880687600}\,I_{45},\\
 \frac43H_{\mathrm E}(k)\pm\nabla_1H_{\mathrm E}(k)
 &\succeq
 \frac{18678478410024061}{90000016149420000}\,I_{45}.
\end{aligned}
\tag{9.10}
\]
The uniform floors are approximately \(0.007040697308619\) and
\(0.20753861176\).
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
At each of the \(256\) quarter-turn points, form \(H\) and its
covariant first jet with exact Gaussian-rational arithmetic.  For
each action and sign, construct a rational approximate eigenbasis,
compute the congruence exactly, and apply
Lemma~\ref{lem:newv3-congruence}.  All \(1024\) matrices are
positive definite.  The worst Wilson point is
\((0,\pi,\pi,0)\) for both signs.  The worst endpoint points for the
plus and minus signs are inversion partners
\((3\pi/2,\pi,\pi,\pi)\) and \((\pi/2,\pi,\pi,\pi)\).
The largest exact Gram-error upper bounds are
\[
 \frac{133501}{500000000000},\qquad
 \frac{133267}{500000000000}.                             \tag{9.11}
\]

The verifier
\[
\texttt{scripts/verify\_ym\_new\_v9\_four\_thirds\_quarter\_grid.py}
\]
has canonical payload SHA--256
\[
\texttt{933A14CE87357FACE49A1E584270F7253730196723EBA14ED085FB81A25AD5BD}.
                                                               \tag{9.12}
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Grid reserve for the original pencil]
\label{cor:newv9-reserve}
At the quarter-turn grid,
\[
 2H_e\pm\nabla_1H_e
 =
 \left(\frac43H_e\pm\nabla_1H_e\right)+\frac23H_e.        \tag{9.13}
\]
Hence the original V99 pencil has both the explicit floor in
(9.10) and an additional positive Hessian reserve.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Equation (9.13) is an identity; apply
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv9-four-thirds}.
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[The inverse in the structural theorem]
\label{fire:newv9-inverse}
Theorem~\ref{thm:newv9-skew} is an exact reduction, not by itself a
noncircular global bound: \(\Omega\) and \(\Pi_{\cal A}\) contain
\(H^{-1}\).  A proof of (9.8) must use an independent Schur,
multiplier, sum-of-squares, or interval certificate.  Substituting
the desired inequality into its own normalized form would be
circular.
\end{firewall}

\begin{decision}[V10 audits companions and searches for a polynomial skew certificate]
\label{dec:newv9-next}
Perform the required V10 companion audit.  Then expand the
inverse-free block (9.9) and the constraint-multiplier completion of
V1 in the same \(64\)-dimensional alias basis.  Search for a sparse
polynomial multiplier that realizes the skew removal before
compression.  Test it first against the stronger \(q=2/3\) grid
floors.

If no polynomial realization exists, use (9.9) as the target for a
one-coordinate block certificate and compare its conditioning with
the V2--V7 direct-pencil hierarchy.  Do not revert to the full or
merely tangential factor-energy gates, both of which have now failed
their necessary unit thresholds.
\end{decision}

\begin{assessment}[Progress in V9]
\label{ass:newv9-progress}
The load-bearing cancellation has been isolated exactly.  The
stronger four-thirds pencil has a complete exact quarter-grid gate,
and its inverse-free block form (9.9) gives a precise algebraic target
for the next multiplier search.  Global positivity between grid
points remains open.
\end{assessment}

\begin{firewall}[Claim boundary after V9]
\label{fire:newv9-final}
No full-torus four-thirds or constant-two covariant Loewner
inequality, continuum response sign, interacting Yang--Mills
measure, reflection-positive regulator removal, exponential
clustering, or Hamiltonian mass gap has yet been proved.
\end{firewall}

\section{V10: companion audit, multiplier anatomy, and four-thirds active circles}
\label{sec:newv10-audit-circles}
